class="feature-banner__content"><div class="feature-banner__text"><h2 class="feature-banner__label">eBook</h2><h3 class="feature-banner__title">The Next Wave in Online Learning</h3><p class="feature-banner__description">There was a time when even the concept of learning far away from your instructor was unthinkable. But here we are today, with online learning having taken the education industry by storm. In this ebook, we discuss the two waves that transformed online learning, or better yet, learning forever and what the future of online learning looks like!</p><p class="feature-banner__description">
